## Per-packet record

Copy these fields under a heading with the packet ID when dispatching it. A subsequent coordinator should be able to reproduce the result from this record.

- **Dispatch** — fresh worker, starting revision, assigned paths, entry point and fixture.
- **TDD evidence** — for each behavior or regression, record the test path/name, command, nonzero exit and observed assertion before implementation, then the green result and the outcome of reruns after any refactor.
- **Implemented** — changed files, published symbols, canonical storage location, schema compatibility and links consumed by the next packet.
- **Independent review** — reviewer identity, integrated revision or diff, clickdummy route, native entry point and fixture. For each due criterion record its expected and actual result, evidence, and verdict.
- **Screenshot comparisons** — the reviewer inserts two clickable Markdown links, **Normal** and **Narrow**, using paths relative to this ledger such as `evidence/V07/<revision>/normal.png` and `evidence/V07/<revision>/narrow.png`. Each PNG shows the clickdummy on the left and the native view on the right. Record the captured state, theme, zoom and actual CSS dimensions of each side. Open both links before sign-off and refresh the pair on a corrected revision.
- **Findings and sign-off** — reproduction, fix revision, independent retest and outcome for each issue. Record the reviewer's final PASS on the integrated revision, or the exact remaining finding or blocker.
- **Integrated** — integrated revision or local diff identifier, targeted green commands, `npm run verify` exit, host check and result. Distinguish native host, harness, adapter fixture and live service.
- **Git checkpoint** — integration branch, base SHA, reviewed candidate ID and exact subject `feat: Vxx <view name>`. Commit this entry with the view, tests and both linked screenshots. The containing commit's SHA is discoverable from the branch history and can be reported after commit.
- **Joins** — producer → consumer, owner, open/closed, reproduction and test. On closure, update the originating record as well.
- **Next action** — outstanding fix, review or commit check for this packet, or the next numbered packet once the accepted checkpoint is verified.

### V01 Project workspace

- **Dispatch** — fresh implementer `v01_implementer`. Starting revision `28f808f7e1adf6eb59f4304649984de6f2159641` on `codex/product-views`. The existing ribbon and host command open the native leaf. Use a disposable blank Vault for creation and recovery, with Kundenportal and a second project for switch, deep link, and search isolation. Reference route `#/project/kundenportal/overview`.
- **Implemented** — project `Project.md` and linked `Backlog.base` under the configured Vault root, with stable `project-<UUID>` identity and versioned, index-backed discovery. The native leaf opens the chooser or restores its scoped route. Search provides scoped lookup through `ProjectSearchContributor` and `resolveProjectReference`. `useNavigationGuard` gives future editors a cancellable leave check. German and English catalogs cover the frame, project actions, and search. The provider adapter checks global `aiEnabled` before invoking a configured CLI. [Project v1 contract](../../contracts/project-v1.md)
- **Integrated candidate** — worktree tree `fa8e3a0a3fe009e21ea2e06e1d90291c36fc55cd` at the V01 base. Git's ordinary index is empty. `AGENTS.md` is the pre-existing local customization. The nine focused test files exited 0 with 46 tests. `npm run verify` exited 0 with 819 tests, 150 files and 85.87% branch coverage. `npm run test:e2e` exited 0 with 20 of 20 specs in Windows Obsidian 1.15.1. The candidate tree matched before and after those commands. Logs are `reports/product-views/V01-integration-focused.log`, `V01-integration-verify.log` and `V01-integration-e2e.log`.
- **Joins** — V03 adds the first real Wissen destination and search records. V04 connects the dirty-editor guard and note return paths. V06 places Domainmodel below Wissen. V07 maps fields and adopts an existing Base. V22 replaces the interim overview at the same route. V23 adds consent settings and V24 adds contextual Copilot.
- **Findings and sign-off** — `v01_reviewer` returned CHANGES REQUIRED on `fa8e3a0a3fe009e21ea2e06e1d90291c36fc55cd`. The separate review entry records the failing narrow-leaf and picker selection comparisons, the search position failure, and the unconnected search contributor.
- **Next action** — `v01_implementer` is reproducing and correcting the four findings. Repeat coordinator verification and independent review on the revised tree before committing.

#### V01 independent review · candidate fa8e3a0

**Verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Fresh reviewer `v01_reviewer` confirmed that all 92 implementation and test blobs match tree `fa8e3a0a3fe009e21ea2e06e1d90291c36fc55cd`. The independent `npm.cmd run verify` exited 0. The integrated candidate also passed 46 focused tests and all 20 native specs. The review opened the actual [clickdummy](../../concept/Product-Engineering-Clickdummy-Story-Maps.html#/project/kundenportal/overview) in Edge and the installed plugin through the Product Engineering open command in Obsidian 1.15.1 on Windows. Both used the Kundenportal and Admin Console names, descriptions, repositories and initial `main` branch from the concept. Native creation started in a copied Vault with no product registrations, then introduced a sentinel file for the collision check.

| Due criterion | Expected and observed evidence | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Project creation, validation and cancellation | The empty workspace and picker both open the form. All three German field labels were present. Submitting an empty form showed the product-name error and focused that field. Cancel left both canonical paths unwritten. UI submission twice in one turn created one Kundenportal registration. Admin Console was created through the picker and received a different UUID. The standalone clickdummy form validated, canceled, created and rediscovered Schichtbuch after browser reload. | PASS |
| Canonical files, collision, branch and repair | Installed Obsidian wrote `Product Engineering/Projects/Kundenportal (2)/Project.md` with `product-engineering.project/v1` and a stable UUID. Its actual `Backlog.base` filters on that UUID. The sentinel in `Kundenportal` survived byte for byte and there was no `Kundenportal (3)/Project.md`. Administration exposed the effective folder, branch and Base. After deleting the Base from the disposable Vault and reopening administration, its repair action restored the original Base bytes. The adapter fixture also asserts that a retry preserves edited Base views. | PASS |
| Project switching, history, reopen and recovery | Native picker switched projects and plugin Back restored the previous project. A record deep link in a separate Obsidian leaf kept its project UUID, `PRD-03` reference and administration origin through reload without `data.json`. The files kept their bytes. The missing record returned to administration and the missing project recovered through the chooser. The integrated native navigation spec independently kept Admin Console in the other leaf while opening Kundenportal. The reference project picker and browser Back restored Kundenportal. | PASS |
| Scoped contributor and lookup contract | The unit fixture supplies two `PRD-03` display IDs and verifies the different results by project, exclusion of an inconsistent route, successful lookup and rejection of an ambiguous stable ID. The installed picker search exposes its own registration and sections. The production palette passes only its local registration contributor to `searchProjectRecords`. The app surface and module screen manifests provide no way to attach the tested contributor to the palette, and the generic record route never consults it. A contributed fixture result cannot be exercised through the native UI as delivered. | CHANGES REQUIRED |
| Expanded/collapsed rail and native theme | Collapsing at 1440 CSS px increased native content width from 1192 to 1360 CSS px and left a focused, named expand control and named overview icon. The reference gained roughly 170 CSS px. No tour, account avatar or Projektkontext surfaced. The native owner had no horizontal overflow at 1440 or 390 CSS px in either host theme before opening an overlay. The native view-state test covers per-leaf rail persistence. | PASS |
| Narrow drawer and project picker | Tab reached the native drawer opener. Enter opened it and focused the picker. Escape dismissed the drawer and restored the opener. Escape from the picker restored its drawer control. The picker itself was 512 CSS px wide in a 390 CSS px split pane. The title and edges clipped and the visual close button fell outside the pane. On desktop its current row was programmatically marked and had the same visual treatment as the other row. The reference showed the current project with an `Aktiv` label. | CHANGES REQUIRED |
| Project search control, command and return | The installed `product-engineering:search-project` command had no default hotkeys. Command and control both opened search with the query focused. Escape restored the trigger. The `Überblick` query and sections filter survived reopening. Keyboard selection of Projektverwaltung opened administration and Back restored the search trigger. With four current results in a 68 CSS px overflow area, reopening reset the list from scroll position 45 to 0. The wider split also confirmed that the search dialog overhangs its owning pane. | CHANGES REQUIRED |
| Dirty navigation hook | The mounted public guard fixture retained the draft, route and history after denied Back and denied navigation, then allowed Back and Forward. The project workspace fixture declined the picker’s create action and kept the current project. The clickdummy live editor retained its draft and focus after Escape and after Weiter bearbeiten. V04 owns attaching the dirty guard to a live editor and testing the complete dirty project-switch and draft-focus sequence. | PASS for V01 hook |
| Global AI consent and right panel | The actual saved `agent-chat` route showed its disabled notice and no textarea with a deliberately unavailable configured CLI and absent consent. The overview had no Copilot control. An explicit fixture opt-in exposed the right-side panel, and explicit opt-out closed it. The configured provider unit test asserts zero factory calls before consent. No external provider was contacted. V23 owns settings UI and the full disable lifecycle. | PASS |

**Screenshot comparisons.** The rejected `normal.png` and `narrow.png` remain in the local archive `reports/product-views/rejected-evidence/V01/fa8e3a0a3fe009e21ea2e06e1d90291c36fc55cd/`. The accepted links are in the retest below. Each side is 1440 × 760 CSS px in Normal and 390 × 760 CSS px in Narrow. Both are German in light mode, 100% zoom and DPR 1, unscaled, with Kundenportal selected on Überblick and the picker open. Narrow opens the project drawer first. The native side captures the owning view surface inside a wide Obsidian window with a second leaf present. The interim overview and the set of live destinations account for background differences. Both rejected files were opened during the review and checked for the intended state, labels, reviewed candidate and visible clipping.

**Reproductions.**

1. In installed Obsidian with a second leaf, constrain the product pane to 390 CSS px. Open Projektnavigation öffnen → Projekt wechseln oder anlegen. The pane spans x=44–434 and the picker spans x=−17–495. Its heading begins outside the left edge and its close button falls beyond the right edge. For search, a second run at 414 CSS px measured both picker and search dialogs at 512 CSS px. The panel and its controls should fit the actual owner. See `v01-native-review-390.log`, `v01-native-split-review.log` and the Narrow comparison.
2. In Kundenportal run project search with the empty query and all four results. Constrain the results list to 68 CSS px in the native host to exercise overflow. Set scrollTop to 45, dismiss with Escape, then reopen with the same trigger. Expected 45. Actual 0. This repro asserted the position before closing and focus after reopening. See `v01-native-ai-review.log`.
3. In the normal picker compare the current Kundenportal row to Admin Console. The same row styling hides the current selection visually. The reference uses a visible `Aktiv` marker. See the Normal comparison.
4. Follow the production palette path in [ProjectSearchDialog](../../../src/presentation/projects/components/ProjectSearchDialog.vue) from `searchProjectRecords` to its contributor argument. Compare with the same-ID application fixture in [projects.test.ts](../../../tests/unit/application/projects.test.ts). The fixture contributes records directly to the helper. There is no contributor list registered by the frame or surface and no contributor lookup when following a generic project record. Supply a contribution point and verify two same-ID contributions through the scoped palette and the resulting routes. V03 still owns the real knowledge contributor and its screens.

**Red and green review.** Checked the candidate assertions against the 16 reported red observations above. They cover start, scoped leaf state, index discovery without settings, duplicate creation, folder reservation replay, empty and picker entry points, denied navigation and create, search and return focus, host command, Base repair, collapsed state, and both AI consent states. The independent native review also checked bytes, two UUIDs, no extra registration, scoped reload, and the actual control labels. The worker report records focused green for same-ID and additional missing-target, collision and Base fidelity branches without a corresponding exact red observation for each. The pre-fix search test asserts query and Back focus but has no overflow result or scroll assertion. The narrow native test asserts owner width before opening search, without measuring the dialog against its owner. The workspace guard test for creation checks canceled navigation and route, without asserting focus or a guarded picker switch. Independent commands and outputs are retained under `reports/product-views`. The five cited clickdummy test files supplied 18 passing reference tests, with the product-flow run filtered to the initial creation journey.

**Next action.** Correct the findings, verify the revised candidate, then repeat the reproductions and both captures. No complete J01–J12 journey is due at V01.

#### V01 repair integration

The implementer fixed the contributor path, overlay geometry, search position, and current-project cue. The verified repair tree is `f6f3ca9d46d0e8ba64ed2d160c171d7e492103d7`. `ProjectSearchPart` exposes a lazy source setup per leaf. The search and generic project record route now share these sources. The live Knowledge source remains V03 scope. Existing navigation guard coverage also checks a refused picker switch, with the live editor and draft-focus integration due in V04.

An earlier runner timeout was adjusted to expose the assertion. | `npm.cmd test -- tests/dom/presentation/projects/contributed-search.test.ts tests/dom/presentation/projects/workspace.test.ts tests/unit/application/projects.test.ts` · exit 0, 12 passed. The mounted frame uses two per-leaf contributor setups, displays PRD-03 for each project’s unique record, retains the saved identity and origin, rejects duplicates and mismatched routes, and leaves the other leaf’s route unchanged through Back. |
| tests/dom/presentation/projects/workspace.test.ts · switches through the project picker and returns to the previous project on Back | `npm.cmd test -- tests/dom/presentation/projects/workspace.test.ts -t 'switches through the project picker'` · exit 1. Job and vision origins produce indexed backlinks and open the actual draft. The note records approval independently of real Git evidence. [Knowledge v1 contract](../../contracts/knowledge-v1.md)
- **Integrated candidate** — tree `c60ed5e87f3eaf73b3571e6510047a5c3671e82a`, 71 implementation and test paths. Implementer `npm run verify` exited 0 with 873 tests and 85.56% branch coverage. The implementer’s exact-tree native suite exited 0 with 24 of 24 specs in Windows Obsidian 1.15.1. Coordinator verification on the same tree exited 0 with 66 focused tests in 15 files, 873 tests in the full gate, and 7 native cases in the Knowledge detail and pop-out specs. Candidate identity matched before and after the checks. Logs are `reports/product-views/V04-integration-focused.log`, `V04-integration-verify.log` and `V04-integration-native.log`.
- **Joins** — V05 supplies verified Git status and history. V06 supplies Domainmodel actions and scope assignments. V12 supplies actual map creation. V24 supplies scoped AI note review. V04 exercised the relevant job/Vault/editing portions of J01/J02. Their whole journeys are due in V05. Tests used copied Vault files and fixtures. No provider, Factory or live Git hosting call was made.

A separate temporary Git 2.48.1.windows.1 repository confirmed literal Unicode/space/bracket paths, exact committed CRLF bytes under its fixture settings, and preservation of independently staged and unstaged bytes. - **Distribution decision** — the integrated Git adapter and history already exceeded the shell’s 1,500,000-byte JavaScript budget before the commit UI was complete. An isolated same-source build measured 1,518,578 bytes with Oxc and 1,549,491 with Terser 5.51.4. Keep Oxc and enforce a 1,600,000-byte `main.js` budget for this increment, with the existing CSS and artifact invariants. Record red and green distribution checks and the final production size on the accepted candidate.
- **Coordinator pre-review finding** — a focused Node assertion on two 600-line Markdown snapshots changed lines 10 and 500. It exited 1 because the first-window `gitDiff` returned only the first addition with `truncated: true` and offered no way to reach the second. The implementer reproduced the missing later addition and navigation. My same-fixture rerun exited 0 with both additions on changed pages 0 and 1.
- **Implemented** — local Git CLI discovery resolves the actual Vault record on the underlying worktree. The Knowledge history tab and shared commit workflow display and compare the saved file, ordered revisions with exact full IDs, and a labeled merge parent. Changed pages stay reachable across long diffs. Commit revalidates the reviewed saved bytes and HEAD, writes one scoped path under Git locking, and preserves unrelated index and worktree bytes. [Git application API](../../../src/application/git/index.ts) · [Shared Git surface](../../../src/presentation/git/index.ts)

The implementer’s full `npm run verify` exited 0 with 903 tests across 172 files and 86.01% branch coverage. Full installed `npm run test:e2e` exited 0 with 27 of 27 specs in Obsidian 1.15.1, including J01 and J02. The Oxc bundle measured 1,522,169 JavaScript bytes under the 1,600,000-byte gate. Coordinator verification on the exact tree exited 0 with 43 focused tests, 903 tests in the full gate and 8 installed cases across the Git and Vault journey specs. Both J01 and J02 passed in the copied Vault. The candidate tree matched before and after the checks. Logs are `reports/product-views/V05-integration-focused.log`, `V05-integration-verify.log` and `V05-integration-journeys-native.log`.

The controlled generic item and immutable-revision fixtures exercised those seams. The native Git process operated on real local temporary fixture repositories. No remote hosting, provider, Factory or CI account was used.

Ordinary Git History re-entry can show a stale working comparison and old revision after an external commit. Both due native journeys passed in this review.

#### V05 repair integration

Repair tree `6c4b1a3bcb3cb28cce6ddba4f0d2046d14720b8e`, 51 implementation and test paths. Revision cards now size to their complete metadata and wrap in a shrinkable timeline track. Opening Git History refreshes the actual local repository, keeps a reachable older selection, and falls back to the working view when it no longer appears in the journal. The current draft and captured commit review retain their inputs.


The initial review covered candidate tree `289b8146736cb32ef3fa6f69ded600684f2e4bcc`. Both original comparisons were frozen before the repairs.

- **Revision metadata fits the timeline rows.** `tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-timeline.e2e.ts` · “shows complete revision metadata at 1440, 860 and 390 pixels with one or two real revisions”. `node.exe node_modules/@wdio/cli/bin/wdio.js run tests/e2e/wdio.conf.mts --spec tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-timeline.e2e.ts` exited 1 on the original installed build. At 1440 px with one real revision it expected zero list overflow and zero row overflow, received 17 px horizontal and 41 px vertical, and saw only the message inside the card. Author, time and short hash extended below its edge. `src/styles/git.css` gives revision cards intrinsic height, normal wrapping and one shrinkable column for the list and card. The same focused command on the rebuilt plugin exited 0, with one and two real rows visible at all three widths. See `V05-review-timeline-red.log` and `V05-review-timeline-green.log`.
- **Ordinary History reentry reads the latest repository state.** `tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-reentry.e2e.ts` · “refreshes Git on ordinary History reentry and keeps a reachable revision and the dirty editor”. `node.exe node_modules/@wdio/cli/bin/wdio.js run tests/e2e/wdio.conf.mts --spec tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-reentry.e2e.ts` exited 1. After a disposable external Git commit of the exact saved file, ordinary History reentry expected `data-git-parent=6d51c991ab427de8e7a9b5a6f87c613252877ec1` and received the stale parent `14c4e9e55a75fa05b17e17157355ef47612e3cf6`. History selection now refreshes the Git journal. A reachable selected revision keeps its comparison. A revision removed from the current history returns selection to the working copy. Refresh replaces the journal while the draft and open commit review retain their existing values. See `V05-review-reentry-red.log`.
- **Same-tree focused tests.** `npm.cmd run test:e2e -- --spec tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-reentry.e2e.ts --spec tests/e2e/specs/knowledge-git-timeline.e2e.ts` exited 0 with both specs and the real Obsidian host. #### V06 repair integration

Repair tree `8764c3c8da12a867e1eaee87a8786e6f1d38494a`, 64 owned paths. Explicitly keeping the original relationship cleans up a verified abandoned staged copy. Meaningful scope edits atomically draft the entity and leave repeated or convergent no-ops alone. The editor displays the latest persisted approval while retaining its fields and conflict base. Native relationship rows use intrinsic height and wrapping within the owner.

**Native contracts** — `storyMapType` owns schema `product-engineering.story-map/v1`, stable `story-map-<uuid>` IDs, project-local `SM-xx` display IDs, scoped `prd_id`/`job_ids` and flat lists for activities, steps, slices, cards and card links. V13 adds the journey editor at that same stable map route and V14 counts PBI-only coverage using the links already owned by the map. ## Journey evidence and final handoff

Use the journey IDs in [acceptance.md](acceptance.md). Record the fixture, integrated revision, test path, environment, actual exit/result and outstanding limitation per journey. Re-run affected journeys when the source, scope or revision semantics change. Record final full verification and real-host results here alongside any pending external capability.
